          errorEvent.target ? Object.getOwnPropertyNames(errorEvent.target) : null,

          p.textContent += "ErrorEvent.target.src = " + errorEvent.target.src + '\n'
        }
      }, true);

            return function () {
              originalMethod.apply(console, arguments);

              if (window.disableLogging) return

              const msg = container.appendChild(document.createElement('pre'));

            };
          })(console[originalMethodName], originalMethodName, container);
